Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any terrestrial module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 886. The projective resolution has length at most 847 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^847(S, F) is trivial. QED.
